#### TPMS_RSA_PARMS {#sec:TPMS-RSA-PARMS}

@tbl:placeholder-table-207 defines the TPMS_RSA_PARMS Structure.

A TPM compatible with this specification and supporting RSA shall
support two primes and an *exponent* of zero. An exponent of zero
indicates that the exponent is the default of $2^{16} + 1$.
Support for other values is optional. Use of other exponents in
duplicated keys is not recommended because the resulting keys would not
be interoperable with other TPMs.

::: Note :::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
Implementations are not required to check that *exponent* is the
default exponent. They may fail to load or generate the key if
*exponent* is not zero.
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::

Table: Definition of (RSA) TPMS_RSA_PARMS Structure {#tbl:placeholder-table-207}

+----------------------------+--------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------+
| Parameter                  | Type                           | Description                                            |
+============================+================================+========================================================+
| symmetric                  | [TPMT_SYM_DEF_OBJECT](         | For a restricted decryption key, shall be set to a     |
|                            | #sec:TPMT-SYM-DEF-OBJECT)+     | supported symmetric algorithm, key size, and mode.     |
|                            |                                |                                                        |
|                            |                                | If the key is not a restricted decryption key, this    |
|                            |                                | field shall be set to TPM_ALG_NULL.                    |
+----------------------------+--------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------+
| scheme                     | [TPMT_RSA_SCHEME](             | scheme.scheme shall be:                                |
|                            | #sec:TPMT-RSA-SCHEME)+         |                                                        |
|                            |                                | for an unrestricted signing key, either TPM_ALG_RSAPSS |
|                            |                                | TPM_ALG_RSASSA or TPM_ALG_NULL                         |
|                            |                                |                                                        |
|                            |                                | for a restricted signing key, either TPM_ALG_RSAPSS or |
|                            |                                | TPM_ALG_RSASSA                                         |
|                            |                                |                                                        |
|                            |                                | for an unrestricted decryption key, TPM_ALG_RSAES,     |
|                            |                                | TPM_ALG_OAEP, or TPM_ALG_NULL unless the object also   |
|                            |                                | has the *sign* attribute                               |
|                            |                                |                                                        |
|                            |                                | for a restricted decryption key, TPM_ALG_NULL          |
|                            |                                |                                                        |
|                            |                                | When both sign and decrypt are SET, restricted shall   |
|                            |                                | be CLEAR and scheme shall be TPM_ALG_NULL.             |
|                            |                                |                                                        |
|                            |                                | ::: Deprecated ::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
|                            |                                | Support for TPM_ALG_NULL except for Storage Keys, and  |
|                            |                                | keys intended for use with the raw RSAEP/RSADP         |
|                            |                                | primitive, was deprecated in version 185. See Part 0.  |
|                            |                                | :::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
+----------------------------+--------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------+
| keyBits                    | [TPMI_RSA_KEY_BITS](           | number of bits in the public modulus                   |
|                            | #sec:TPMI-RSA-KEY-BITS)        |                                                        |
+----------------------------+--------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------+
| exponent                   | UINT32                         | the public exponent                                    |
|                            |                                |                                                        |
|                            |                                | an odd number greater than 2.                          |
+----------------------------+--------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------+


#### TPMS_ECC_PARMS {#sec:TPMS-ECC-PARMS}

This @tbl:placeholder-table-208 structure contains the parameters for prime modulus ECC.

Table: Definition of (ECC) TPMS_ECC_PARMS Structure {#tbl:placeholder-table-208}

+--------------------+----------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+
| Parameter          | Type                             | Description                                                  |
+====================+==================================+==============================================================+
| symmetric          | [TPMT_SYM_DEF_OBJECT](           | For a restricted decryption key, shall be set to a supported |
|                    | #sec:TPMT-SYM-DEF-OBJECT)+       | symmetric algorithm, key size. and mode.                     |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | if the key is not a restricted decryption key, this field    |
|                    |                                  | shall be set to TPM_ALG_NULL.                                |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | The TPM will return `TPM_RC_SYMMETRIC` if any of the above   |
|                    |                                  | requirements are not met.                                    |
+--------------------+----------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+
| scheme             | [TPMT_ECC_SCHEME](               | If the *sign* attribute of the key is SET, then this shall   |
|                    | #sec:TPMT-ECC-SCHEME)+           | be a valid signing scheme.                                   |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | If the sign parameter in curveID indicates a mandatory       |
|                    |                                  | scheme, then this field shall have the same value.           |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | If the *decrypt* attribute of the key is SET, then this      |
|                    |                                  | shall be a valid key exchange scheme or `TPM_ALG_NULL`.      |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | If the key is a Storage Key, then this field shall be        |
|                    |                                  | `TPM_ALG_NULL`.                                              |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | If the key is not a Storage Key, and *curveID* is either     |
|                    |                                  | `TPM_ECC_CURVE_25519` or `TPM_ECC_CURVE_448`, then this      |
|                    |                                  | field shall not be `TPM_ALG_NULL`.                           |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | The TPM will return `TPM_RC_SCHEME` if any of the above      |
|                    |                                  | requirements are not met.                                    |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | ::: Deprecated ::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
|                    |                                  | Support for TPM_ALG_NULL except for Storage Keys was         |
|                    |                                  | deprecated in version 185. See Part 0.                       |
|                    |                                  | :::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
+--------------------+----------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+
| curveID            | [TPMI_ECC_CURVE](                | ECC curve ID                                                 |
|                    | #sec:TPMI-ECC-CURVE)             |                                                              |
+--------------------+----------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+
| kdf                | [TPMT_KDF_SCHEME](               | if the key is an unrestricted decryption `TPM_ALG_ECDH` key, |
|                    | #sec:TPMT-KDF-SCHEME)+           | an optional key derivation scheme                            |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | Shall be NULL in all other cases (`TPM_RC_KDF`).             |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | If this field is not NULL, then this key can be used with    |
|                    |                                  | `TPM2_Encapsulate()` and `TPM2_Decapsulate()` (as well as    |
|                    |                                  | `TPM2_ECDH_KeyGen()` and `TPM2_ECDH_ZGen()`). In the context |
|                    |                                  | of `TPM2_Encapsulate()` and `TPM2_Decapsulate()`, the        |
|                    |                                  | KEM is equivalent to DHKEM(*curveID*, *kdf*) from RFC        |
|                    |                                  | 9180 [@{rfc9180}]                                            |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | ::: Note ::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
|                    |                                  | Currently, `TPM_ALG_HKDF` is the only supported KDF for      |
|                    |                                  | DHKEM.                                                       |
|                    |                                  | :::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | In the context of object creation, `TPM2_LoadExternal()`, or |
|                    |                                  | `TPM2_TestParms()`, `TPM_RC_KDF` indicates the TPM does not  |
|                    |                                  | support the requested KDF.                                   |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | ::: Note ::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
|                    |                                  | In the context of `TPM2_Encapsulate()` and                   |
|                    |                                  | `TPM2_Decapsulate()`, *scheme*.*details*.*ecdh*.*hashAlg*    |
|                    |                                  | is ignored, because *kdf* specifies all parameters of the    |
|                    |                                  | KDF, including any hash algorithm.                           |
|                    |                                  | :::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
|                    |                                  |                                                              |
|                    |                                  | If this field is NULL, then this key cannot be used with     |
|                    |                                  | `TPM2_Encapsulate()` and `TPM2_Decapsulate()`.               |
+--------------------+----------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+

#### TPMS_MLDSA_PARMS {#sec:TPMS-MLDSA-PARMS}

@tbl:tpms-mldsa-parms contains the parameters for ML-DSA keys.

Table: Definition of (MLDSA) TPMS_MLDSA_PARMS Structure {#tbl:tpms-mldsa-parms}

+-----------------------+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+
| Parameter             | Type                          | Description                                                  |
+=======================+===============================+==============================================================+
| parameterSet          | [TPMI_MLDSA_PARMS](           | ML-DSA parameter set ID                                      |
|                       | #sec:TPMI-MLDSA-PARMS)        |                                                              |
+-----------------------+-------------------------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+
| allowExternalMu       | [TPMI_YES_NO](                | If YES, this key can be used with                            |
|                       | #sec:TPMI-YES-NO)             | `TPM2_VerifyDigestSignature()` and `TPM2_SignDigest()`.      |
|                       |                               | In the context of these two commands, the                    |
|                       |                               | *digest* value will be interpreted as the 512-byte external  |
|                       |                               | Mu ($\mu$) value as computed in Algorithm 7                  |
|                       |                               | (`ML-DSA.Sign_internal`), Line 6 of FIPS 204 [@{fips204}].   |
|                       |                               |                                                              |
|                       |                               | If NO, this key cannot be used with                          |
|                       |                               | `TPM2_VerifyDigestSignature()` and `TPM2_SignDigest()`.      |
|                       |                               |                                                              |
|                       |                               | If a TPM supports ML-DSA but not external Mu, it will return |
|                       |                               | `TPM_RC_EXT_MU` in object creation and `TPM2_TestParms()`    |
|                       |                               | if *allowExternalMu* is YES.                                 |

|                       |                               | :::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |

### TPM2B_PUBLIC {#sec:TPM2B-PUBLIC}

This @tbl:placeholder-table-212 sized buffer is used to embed a `TPMT_PUBLIC` in a load
command and in any response that returns a public area.

Table: Definition of TPM2B_PUBLIC Structure {#tbl:placeholder-table-212}

+------------------------+-----------------------+---------------------------------------------------------------------+
| Parameter              | Type                  | Description                                                         |
+========================+=======================+=====================================================================+
| size=                  | UINT16                | size of publicArea                                                  |
|                        |                       |                                                                     |
|                        |                       | ::: Note :::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
|                        |                       | The "=" will force the TPM to try to unmarshal a TPMT_PUBLIC        |
|                        |                       | and check that the unmarshaled size matches the value of *size.* If |
|                        |                       | all the required fields of a TPMT_PUBLIC are not present, the TPM   |
|                        |                       | will return an error (generally TPM_RC_SIZE) when attempting to     |
|                        |                       | unmarshal the TPMT_PUBLIC.                                          |
|                        |                       | ::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
+------------------------+-----------------------+---------------------------------------------------------------------+
| publicArea             | +[TPMT_PUBLIC](       | the public area                                                     |
|                        | #sec:TPMT-PUBLIC)     |                                                                     |

|                        |                       | The "+" indicates that the caller can specify that use of           |
|                        |                       | TPM_ALG_NULL is allowed for *nameAlg.*                              |
|                        |                       | ::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: |
+------------------------+-----------------------+---------------------------------------------------------------------+


### TPM2B_TEMPLATE {#sec:TPM2B-TEMPLATE}

This @tbl:placeholder-table-213 sized buffer is used to embed a `TPMT_PUBLIC` for
`TPM2_CreateLoaded()`.

Unmarshaling of this structure is fairly complex due to requirements for
backwards compatibility. Unlike a `TPM2B_PUBLIC`, this structure is
unmarshaled as an array of bytes that is passed to the action code. The
action code will then unmarshal the embedded structure.

If the parent is not a derivation parent, this structure is unmarshaled
normally. If the parent is a derivation parent, *unique* is unmarshaled
as a TPMS_DERIVE Structure (*label* and *context*). See @sec:TPMU-PUBLIC-ID.

Table: Definition of TPM2B_TEMPLATE Structure {#tbl:placeholder-table-213}

+--------------------------------------------+---------------+---------------------------------------------------------+
| Parameter                                  | Type          | Description                                             |
+============================================+===============+=========================================================+
| size                                       | UINT16        | size of publicArea                                      |
+--------------------------------------------+---------------+---------------------------------------------------------+
| buffer\[size\]{:sizeof([TPMT_PUBLIC](      | BYTE          | the public area                                         |
| #sec:TPMT-PUBLIC))}                        |               |                                                         |
+--------------------------------------------+---------------+---------------------------------------------------------+


## Private Area Structures {#sec:private-area-structures}

### Introduction

The structures in @sec:private-area-structures define the contents and construction of the
private portion of a TPM object. A `TPM2B_PRIVATE` along with a
`TPM2B_PUBLIC` are needed to describe a TPM object.

A `TPM2B_PRIVATE` area may be encrypted by different symmetric algorithms
or, in some cases, not encrypted at all.

### Sensitive Data Structures {#sec:sensitive-data-structures}

#### Introduction

The structures in @sec:sensitive-data-structures define the presumptive internal representations
of the sensitive areas of the various entities. A TPM may store the
sensitive information in any desired format but when constructing a
`TPM_PRIVATE`, the formats in @sec:sensitive-data-structures shall be used.

#### TPM2B_PRIVATE_VENDOR_SPECIFIC {#sec:TPM2B-PRIVATE-VENDOR-SPECIFIC}

This @tbl:placeholder-table-214 structure is defined for coding purposes. For IO to the
TPM, the sensitive portion of the key will be in a canonical form. For
an RSA key, this will be one of the prime factors of the public modulus.
After loading, it is typical that other values will be computed so that
computations using the private key will not need to start with just one
prime factor. This structure can be used to store the results of such
vendor-specific calculations.

The value for `PRIVATE_VENDOR_SPECIFIC_BYTES` is determined by the
vendor.

Table: Definition of TPM2B_PRIVATE_VENDOR_SPECIFIC Structure {#tbl:placeholder-table-214}

+--------------------------------------------------------------+-----------------------+-------------------------------+
| Parameter                                                    | Type                  | Description                   |
+==============================================================+=======================+===============================+
| size                                                         | UINT16                |                               |
+--------------------------------------------------------------+-----------------------+-------------------------------+
| buffer\[size\]{:PRIVATE_VENDOR_SPECIFIC_BYTES}               | BYTE                  |                               |
+--------------------------------------------------------------+-----------------------+-------------------------------+


#### TPMU_SENSITIVE_COMPOSITE {#sec:TPMU-SENSITIVE-COMPOSITE}

@tbl:placeholder-table-215 defines the `TPMU_SENSITIVE_COMPOSITE` union.

Table: Definition of TPMU_SENSITIVE_COMPOSITE Union  {#tbl:placeholder-table-215}

+--------------+----------------------------------------------+-------------------------------+------------------------+
| Parameter    | Type                                         | Selector                      | Description            |
+==============+==============================================+===============================+========================+
| rsa          | [TPM2B_PRIVATE_KEY_RSA](                     | TPM_ALG_RSA                   | a prime factor of the  |
|              | #sec:TPM2B-PRIVATE-KEY-RSA)                  |                               | public key             |
+--------------+----------------------------------------------+-------------------------------+------------------------+
| ecc          | [TPM2B_ECC_PARAMETER](                       | TPM_ALG_ECC                   | the integer private key|
|              | #sec:TPM2B-ECC-PARAMETER)                    |                               |                        |
+--------------+----------------------------------------------+-------------------------------+------------------------+
| bits         | [TPM2B_SENSITIVE_DATA](                      | TPM_ALG_KEYEDHASH             | the private data       |
|              | #sec:TPM2B-SENSITIVE-DATA)                   |                               |                        |
+--------------+----------------------------------------------+-------------------------------+------------------------+
| sym          | [TPM2B_SYM_KEY](#sec:TPM2B-SYM-KEY)          | TPM_ALG_SYMCIPHER             | the symmetric key      |
+--------------+----------------------------------------------+-------------------------------+------------------------+
| mldsa        | [TPM2B_PRIVATE_KEY_MLDSA](                   | TPM_ALG_MLDSA or              | the private seed Xi    |
|              | #sec:TPM2B-PRIVATE-KEY-MLDSA)                | TPM_ALG_HASH_MLDSA            | ($\xi$)                |
+--------------+----------------------------------------------+-------------------------------+------------------------+
